# Questions and their numbers

Generated by `scripts/reported/emit_reported_values.py` from the question
list (`src/continuum_swr_replay/reported/questions.py`) and the summaries
(`paper/reported/<summary>.json`); do not edit by hand. Cite a value in
`paper/main.tex` by its macro. A value marked *pending* may not be quoted
as a finding. A summary file is named for what it holds; a question's part
is where the paper reports it. *Builder function* names the function in
`src/continuum_swr_replay/reported/<summary>.py`, or in a module of the
package `reported/<summary>/`, that names and describes a value. The
statistic is computed by the `analysis/` function it calls, or copied from
an input file; the builder itself only counts the size of an input. The
last section lists, for each builder function, the `analysis/` functions
it calls.

### 1.2. On simulated activity that is not one clean replay (rest with nothing added, events that represent no place, events whose place jumps, replays in two parts), what does each detector report?

Status: computed. Values: 34.

*Note:* The controls sweep. No figure shows it; it holds the simulation's false-positive evidence.

Sources:

- paper/reported/inputs/detection/detection_controls_summary.csv (scripts/detection/sweep_memoryless_controls.py)

### 2a.2. Do held-out likelihood and event-label stability favor the four-state model over the Continuous–Fragmented and reduced models?

Status: dropped; not needed: the paper does not claim the four-state model is better than a reduced one. Values: 0.

### 2a.4. How often does the detector report non-local events on shuffled or thinned real spikes, and in windows where no replay is expected?

Status: dropped; wrongly framed as a false-positive rate: shuffles, thinned spikes and quiet windows are not known negatives; the held-out state test (question 2a.7) and the matched windows of question 2c.3 take its place. Values: 0.

### 3.4. Does content correlate with the upcoming choice once behavior is in the model, with or without SWR?

### 3.9. Are SWR and non-SWR events recruited differently by behavior?

Status: dropped; not shown as a gross recruitment difference (the peri-switch non-SWR profile is a null); superseded by the per-effect questions 3.1–3.7. Values: 0.

### 3.10. Does replay content predict the upcoming choice?

Status: dropped; invalidated by task autocorrelation; the one surviving thread, the non-SWR replay_frac term of question 3.4, is null over the behavioral baseline. Values: 0.
